Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on cosmology structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 754. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 114 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 754 + O(1/n^114). Summing over all modes and applying the cosmology convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the cosmology space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 276 and the convergence rate is O(462/n^3).

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to cosmology theory, we decompose the problem into 276 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 462. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
